	<span class="angle-icon"></span>

											<div class="facet-content rte">
												<p data-start="410" data-end="939">DU-BRO R/C Protective Foam Rubber is an essential material for anyone looking to protect delicate electronics inside their RC airplanes, cars, drones, and boats. This high-quality foam rubber is designed to absorb shock and vibration, helping your sensitive components—such as receivers, batteries, gyro units, ignition modules, and flight controllers—stay safe and perform reliably. If your RC model operates on rough terrain, grass fields, or experiences heavy vibration from engines or motors, this foam rubber is a must-have.</p>
<p data-start="941" data-end="1333">The foam is soft yet durable, offering excellent cushioning without adding unnecessary weight to your model. It can be easily cut to the size you need, making it perfect for custom installations or tight spaces. Simply place it under or around your components to create a protective buffer that reduces the chances of damage, electrical failures, or interference caused by constant vibration.</p>
<p data-start="1335" data-end="1622">DU-BRO R/C Protective Foam Rubber is ideal for:<br data-start="1382" data-end="1385">– Cushioning receivers and gyro units<br data-start="1422" data-end="1425">– Protecting ignition systems in gas and glow aircraft<br data-start="1479" data-end="1482">– Reducing vibration in drones and multirotors<br data-start="1528" data-end="1531">– Mounting batteries in RC cars and boats<br data-start="1572" data-end="1575">– General shock absorption for RC electronics</p>
<p data-start="1624" data-end="1853">Hobbyists appreciate how easy this foam is to work with and how much stability it adds to their setups. It’s especially useful for older models or high-power aircraft where vibration can lead to signal glitches or component wear.</p>
<p data-start="1855" data-end="2060">If you want simple, dependable protection for your RC electronics, <strong data-start="1922" data-end="1959">DU-BRO R/C Protective Foam Rubber</strong> provides the cushioning and vibration control your model needs to stay safe and perform at its best.</p>
<p>High quality latex foam rubber. Works great for protecting your receiver and fuel tank from vibration and also gives protection during a crash.</p>
<ul>
<li><span style="line-height: 1.4;">Available in two sizes: 1/4" (6.3 mm) and 1/2" (12.7 mm).</span></li>
<li><span style="line-height: 1.4;">Each piece measures: 7" (177.8 mm) X 11" (279.4 mm). (QTY/PKG: 1 )</span></li>
<li><strong style="line-height: 1.4;">Made in USA</strong></li>
</ul>
<p><em>*Note: Color may vary by shipment.¬† Material and quality does not change.</em></p>

<script>
  // Handle loader image errors and implement fallback logic
  window.handleLoaderError = function (img, type) {
    const loaderContent = document.getElementById('byobLoaderContent');
    if (!loaderContent) return;

    if (type === 'svg' && '' !== '') {
      // SVG failed, try GIF fallback
      const gifImg = document.createElement('img');
      gifImg.src = '';
      gifImg.alt = 'Loading...';
      gifImg.className = 'byob-loader-image';
      gifImg.id = 'byobLoaderGif';
      gifImg.onerror = function () {
        handleLoaderError(this, 'gif');
      };

      // Replace failed SVG with GIF
      loaderContent.innerHTML = '';
      loaderContent.appendChild(gifImg);
    } else {
      // Both custom loaders failed or no fallback available, use default spinner
      loaderContent.innerHTML = '<div class="byob-loader-spinner" id="byobLoaderSpinner"></div>';
    }
  };
</script>
